Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a condensed_matter algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 141. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 847/3.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 141 + O(1/n^847). Summing over all modes and applying the condensed_matter convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
